  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子冷却对可靠的量子计算至关重要，但在冷却性能与实现资源之间存在权衡。尽管储库记忆可以改善冷却性能的某些方面，但其相关的资源成本（特别是电路实现成本）尚未被充分理解。因此需要系统研究储库记忆如何影响单量子比特热浴算法冷却中冷却性能与几何实现成本之间的权衡。

Method: 采用伪模映射（pseudomode mapping）方法将非马尔可夫阻尼Jaynes-Cummings动力学表示为重复碰撞电路，并评估其几何实现成本。通过基于矩阵的模拟仿真和在IBM Kawasaki Heron r2量子处理器上的实际实现，系统分析储库记忆对冷却步数和几何协议成本的影响。

Result: 研究发现了一个明确的权衡关系：抑制储库记忆效应可以减少所需的冷却步数，但通常会增加几何协议的实现成本。这一权衡关系在矩阵模拟和IBM量子处理器的实际实验中均得到验证。

Conclusion: 本研究为算法冷却中的储库工程提供了基于资源的新视角，揭示了储库记忆在冷却性能优化与电路实现资源消耗之间所扮演的关键角色。这对于在实际量子计算系统中设计高效且资源友好的量子冷却方案具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Quantum cooling is important for reliable quantum computation but involves a trade-off between cooling performance and implementation resources. Although reservoir memory can improve particular aspects of cooling performance, the associated resource cost, particularly for circuit implementation, remains insufficiently understood. Here, we investigate how reservoir memory affects the trade-off between cooling performance quantified by the cooling-step count and geometric implementation cost in single-qubit heat-bath algorithmic cooling. Using a pseudomode mapping, we represent the non-Markovian damped Jaynes--Cummings dynamics by a repeated collision circuit and evaluate its geometric implementation cost. Using matrix-based simulations and an implementation on the ibm_kawasaki Heron r2 processor, we identify a trade-off: suppressing reservoir memory reduces the cooling-step count but generally increases the geometric protocol cost. Our work provides a resource-based perspective on reservoir engineering for algorithmic cooling.

</details>


### [98] [Entanglement-enabled Criticality in One-dimensional Quantum Contact Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30681)
*Ya-Xin Xiang,Tianyi Yan,Weibin Li,Yu-Qiang Ma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究通过多种数值方法证明一维量子接触过程(QCP)经历连续吸收态相变,其临界指数不同于经典情况,并揭示了即使微弱的量子纠缠也对正确描述临界行为至关重要,而半经典方法会错误地预测一阶相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维量子接触过程的相变性质一直存在争议。尽管量子接触过程作为经典接触过程的量子推广受到持续关注,但其相变的本质、临界行为以及量子效应(特别是量子纠缠)在其中的作用尚未明确。需要通过系统性研究来澄清一维QCP的相变类型,并理解量子特性如何影响其临界行为。

Method: 研究采用多种互补的数值模拟方法:1) Liouvillian谱分析用于研究系统的能谱性质;2) 张量跳跃方法(tensor jump method)处理量子多体动力学;3) 精确量子跳跃蒙特卡罗(exact quantum jump Monte Carlo)模拟开放量子系统演化;4) 截断Wigner模拟作为半经典近似方法进行对比。通过这些方法系统地分析了一维QCP的相变特征、临界指数以及量子纠缠的作用。

Result: 研究确认一维QCP经历连续的吸收态相变,其临界指数与经典接触过程明显不同。发现Liouvillian能隙在远低于临界点处就已闭合,表明单纯的谱隙分析无法区分真正的相变和亚稳态。一维QCP虽然仅具有弱纠缠,但这种弱纠缠对于正确捕捉临界行为是不可或缺的。相比之下,忽略量子纠缠的半经典方法(如截断Wigner模拟)人为地稳定了活跃态,错误地预测出一阶相变。

Conclusion: 本工作确立了一维量子接触过程相变的量子起源,证明了即使是微弱的量子纠缠也在耗散量子多体系统中扮演着关键角色。研究阐明了一维QCP的连续相变性质及其独特的临界指数,揭示了量子相干性和纠缠对于正确描述开放量子系统非平衡相变的本质重要性,并强调了在研究此类系统时需要超越半经典近似的必要性。

Abstract: The contact process is a paradigmatic example of nonequilibrium dynamics, with broad applications ranging from chemistry to sociology. Its quantum counterpart, the quantum contact process (QCP), extends the classical model to include coherent processes. Despite sustained interest, the nature of the transition in the one-dimensional (1D) QCP remains debatable. Here, combining Liouvillian spectral analysis, the tensor jump method, exact quantum jump Monte Carlo, and truncated Wigner simulations, we show that 1D QCP undergoes a continuous absorbing-state phase transition, with critical exponents distinct from the classical case. We further find Liouvillian gap closes well below the critical point, highlighting that spectral gap analysis alone cannot distinguish a phase transition from metastability in the QCP. Crucially, the 1D QCP is weakly entangled, yet even this weak entanglement is indispensable for capturing the correct critical behavior, whereas semiclassical methods artificially stabilize the active state and predict a spurious first-order transition. Our work establishes the quantum origin of the phase transition in the 1D QCP and underscores the essential role of entanglement in dissipative quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [99] [Flatness-Preserving Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30697)
*Otto Veltheim,Esko Keski-Vakkuri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子态的"平坦性"(flatness)及其对立性质"反平坦性"(antiflatness)的资源理论,解决了保平坦操作(FPOs)的刻画这一开放问题,证明了所有保正交操作(OPOs)都是等距嵌入,并完整刻画了保平坦操作的数学结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子资源理论中,平坦态(与投影算子成比例的量子态)及其反性质"反平坦性"的研究日益受到关注。建立反平坦性的完整资源理论需要明确其自由操作,即保平坦操作(FPOs)的数学刻画,但这一问题尚未解决。为此,本文旨在完整刻画保平坦操作的结构,并探讨其与保正交操作(OPOs)的关系。

Method: 本文采用量子信息理论和算子理论方法。首先研究保正交操作(OPOs),通过严格的数学证明表明所有OPOs都是等距嵌入,可表示为幺正变换/等距映射与固定态附加的组合。在此基础上,利用平坦态的数学性质,通过构造性证明和分类讨论方法,刻画保平坦操作的完整结构。同时,研究平坦态的凸组合性质,证明关于正交平坦态凸组合的唯一性定理。

Result: 研究得到以下主要结果:(1)所有保正交操作(OPOs)都是等距嵌入,可表示为幺正/等距变换与附加固定态的组合;(2)所有保平坦操作(FPOs)要么是映射到某个固定平坦态的常数映射,要么是一类特殊的OPO,其中附加的固定态必须是平坦态;(3)平坦态的凸组合只有在正交平坦态之间才可能保持平坦性,且权重由各态的纯度决定。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了量子态平坦性资源理论中保平坦操作的刻画问题,建立了FPOs与OPOs之间的明确联系,证明了FPOs的结构定理。研究表明保平坦操作具有严格的数学约束:除平凡的常数映射外,只能是附加平坦态的等距嵌入。这些结果为反平坦性资源理论奠定了操作论基础,对理解量子态的几何结构和量子资源转换具有重要理论意义。

Abstract: A quantum state is called flat if it is proportional to a projector. There has been recent interest in studying antiflatness, the property of diverging from flat states, and establishing a resource theory for it. Identifying the free operations (the Flatness-Preserving Operations (FPOs)) remained an open problem. For this purpose, we first discuss Orthogonality-Preserving Operations (OPOs), of which trivial examples are unitary operations in an isolated system. More generally, we give a simple proof that all OPOs are isometric embeddings consisting of combinations of unitaries/isometries and appending a fixed state. We then show that all FPOs are either constant maps to some fixed flat state or a special case of an OPO, where the appended fixed state must be a flat state. We also show that the only possible flat convex combinations of flat states are those of orthogonal flat states with weights given by their purities.

</details>


### [100] [PauLie: Fast Classification of Pauli Dynamical Lie Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30771)
*Oxana Shaya,Konstantin Golovkin,Mainak Roy,Vincent Russo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了PauLie框架,一个开源工具,用于高效判定由任意泡利串生成的动力学李代数(DLA)的同构类型,将计算复杂度从指数级降低到多项式级O(n|G|max(n,|G|)),使DLA分类成为常规预处理步骤,并在多个量子算法应用中展示了其实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式计算动力学李代数(DLA)一直是量子系统研究中的主要计算瓶颈,传统的暴力李闭包方法的复杂度随量子比特数n呈指数增长。然而,许多应用只需要知道DLA的同构类型而非完整结构,这为开发更高效的算法提供了机会。因此需要一种能够快速判定DLA同构类型的方法,以突破这一计算瓶颈。

Method: 研究者开发了PauLie开源框架,该方法基于Aguilar等人提出的反对易图约化技术。PauLie能够判定由任意泡利串生成的动力学李代数的同构类型,算法时间复杂度为O(n|G|max(n,|G|)),其中n是量子比特数,|G|是生成元的数量。该方法通过利用泡利算符的代数结构和图论工具,避免了显式构造完整的李代数闭包。

Result: PauLie将DLA分类从计算密集型任务转变为常规预处理步骤。研究团队在多个应用场景中验证了该框架的有效性:作为李代数模拟路由和嘉当分解的结构预言器;用于诊断变分量子算法中的贫瘠高原问题;以及用于构造具有最优生成率的通用泡利串生成元集合。这些应用证明了PauLie在实际量子算法研究中的广泛实用性。

Conclusion: PauLie框架成功解决了动力学李代数计算的效率瓶颈,通过多项式时间算法实现了DLA同构类型的快速判定。该工具为量子系统的可控性分析、表达能力评估和模拟复杂度研究提供了高效的计算基础,并在变分量子算法优化、量子模拟和生成元集合设计等多个领域展现了重要的应用价值,为量子计算研究提供了实用的结构分析工具。

Abstract: The dynamical Lie algebra (DLA) governs the controllability, expressibility, and simulation complexity of a quantum system. Explicitly computing it has been a major computational bottleneck: brute-force Lie closure scales exponentially in the number of qubits $n$. Many applications, however, consult only the isomorphism type of the DLA. We introduce PauLie, an open-source framework that decides this isomorphism type for DLAs generated by arbitrary Pauli strings, building on the anticommutation-graph reduction of Aguilar et al. PauLie runs in $O(n|\mathcal{G}|\max(n,|\mathcal{G}|))$ time, where $|\mathcal{G}|$ is the number of generators, turning DLA classification into a routine preprocessing step. We demonstrate its use as a structural oracle for routing Lie-algebraic simulation and Cartan decomposition, diagnosing barren plateaus in variational quantum algorithms, and engineering universal Pauli string generator sets with optimal generation rate.

</details>


### [101] [Quantum Block Turbo Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30775)
*Khaled Jebari,Luiz Anet Neto,Ramesh Pyndiah,Jean-Louis de Bougrenet de la Tocnaye*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了量子分组Turbo码理论,这是经典Turbo乘积码的量子模拟版本,并描述了其迭代译码算法,在去极化信道上对不同组成码进行了性能仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Turbo码在90年代初彻底改变了纠错理论,主要应用于卷积Turbo码和Turbo乘积码两类。卷积Turbo码已被成功改造应用于量子纠错领域,形成了量子串行Turbo码理论,但Turbo乘积码的量子版本尚未建立。因此本文旨在填补这一空白,发展量子分组Turbo码(即Turbo乘积码的量子模拟)理论。

Method: 本文建立了量子分组Turbo码的理论框架,将经典Turbo乘积码的构造方法扩展到量子纠错领域。设计了适用于量子分组Turbo码的迭代译码算法,该算法能够处理量子信道中的错误。针对去极化信道模型,采用不同的组成码(constituent codes)构建量子分组Turbo码,并通过仿真评估其纠错性能。

Result: 通过在去极化信道上的仿真实验,验证了量子分组Turbo码及其迭代译码算法的有效性。仿真结果展示了使用不同组成码时量子分组Turbo码的性能表现,证明了该编码方案在量子纠错中的可行性。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了量子分组Turbo码理论,完成了经典Turbo乘积码向量子纠错领域的推广。提出的迭代译码算法能够有效处理量子信道错误,仿真结果验证了该方案的性能。这项工作为量子通信和量子计算中的错误校正提供了新的理论工具和技术方案,丰富了量子Turbo码家族。

Abstract: In the early nineties, the introduction of turbo codes revolutionized classical error correction. The idea was mainly applied on two types of codes: convolutional turbo codes and turbo product codes. The first type of codes was adapted to quantum error correction which initiated the theory of quantum serial turbo codes. In this paper, we present a theory for quantum block turbo codes, the quantum analog of the second type of turbo codes. We describe their iterative decoding algorithm and simulate their performances on a depolarizing channel for different constituent codes.

</details>


### [102] [Neutral atom quantum computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30783)
*M. Saffman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了中性原子量子比特作为大规模量子计算实现方案的发展历程,从25年前的最初提案到近年来在性能、规模、量子算法和容错纠错方面的快速进展,并对未来发展进行了展望。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子量子比特是实现大规模量子计算机的主要途径之一。近年来该技术在性能和规模上取得了快速发展,已实现量子算法演示和用于容错纠错的逻辑量子比特。需要系统性地回顾这一领域的物理基础、当前能力和未来发展方向。

Method: 本文采用综述性研究方法,系统梳理了中性原子量子比特的物理原理,调研了当前技术能力和最新进展,并基于现状对未来发展趋势进行分析和展望。

Result: 文章全面呈现了中性原子量子比特从概念提出(25年前)、通用门集首次演示(15年前)到近年来的快速发展轨迹,包括量子算法的成功演示以及容错纠错逻辑量子比特的实现等重要里程碑。

Conclusion: 中性原子量子比特技术已经从理论概念发展为具有实际应用潜力的量子计算平台,在阵列规模、操控性能、量子算法实现和容错纠错方面均取得显著进展,展现出实现大规模量子计算机的良好前景。

Abstract: Neutral atom qubits are one of the leading approaches for implementation of a large scale quantum computer. The original proposals for neutral atom qubits were formulated more than 25 years ago, with first demonstrations of a universal gate set following 10 years later. In the last few years the performance and scale of neutral atom qubit arrays has developed at a rapid pace leading to demonstrations of quantum algorithms, and logical qubits for fault tolerant error correction. This contribution reviews the physics of the neutral atom approach, surveys current capabilities, and provides an outlook for future progress.

</details>


### [103] [Photon-efficient quantum repeater chains via hyperentanglement-assisted purification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30786)
*Vinay Kumar,Krishan Joshi,Claudio Cicconetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了DAHR(自由度辅助超纠缠中继器)协议,通过在量子中继链的每个段中整合DAEPP(自由度辅助纠缠纯化协议)与BDCZ方案,实现了比传统BBPSSW纯化方案更高效的纠缠分发,仅用单光子对即可达到传统方案需要2-3轮纯化的性能,并在低操作可靠性条件下表现出显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于Werner态纯化(BBPSSW协议)和纠缠交换(BDCZ方案)的线性量子中继链存在严格的操作可靠性阈值限制,且需要消耗大量资源进行递归纯化(每轮消耗两个纠缠对才能概率性地产生一个纯化态)。为克服这些局限性,需要开发一种更高效、资源消耗更少的量子纠缠分发方案,利用光子的多个自由度(如偏振和空间模式)来提高纯化效率。

Method: 提出DAHR(自由度辅助超纠缠中继器)协议,将DAEPP(自由度辅助纠缠纯化协议)整合到BDCZ方案中。DAEPP通过消耗某些自由度来提纯其他自由度的保真度。具体方法包括:(1)在端到端路径的每个段中集成DAEPP与BDCZ方案;(2)推导嵌入单段DAEPP的BDCZ方案的闭式端到端保真度递归公式;(3)给出利用BBPSSW纯化的BDCZ基准方案匹配DAHR每段有效保真度所需的严格资源下界;(4)在基于先前实验的代表性非对称操作点进行数值分析。

Result: 数值分析显示,在代表性非对称操作点下,传统BBPSSW纯化方案需要2-3轮纯化才能匹配DAHR单光子对的性能。更重要的是,在临界操作可靠性以下,无论进行多少轮BBPSSW纯化都无法达到DAHR一轮DAEPP的性能表现。这表明DAHR在资源效率和操作可靠性容忍度方面具有显著优势。

Conclusion: DAHR协议通过利用超纠缠的多自由度特性,成功突破了传统量子中继方案的资源消耗和操作可靠性限制。该方案在保持高保真度的同时大幅减少了所需的纠缠对数量,特别是在低操作可靠性环境下展现出传统方案无法企及的性能优势,为实用化量子网络中的高效纠缠分发提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Linear quantum repeater chains based on Werner-state purification (the BBPSSW protocol) and entanglement swapping (the BDCZ scheme) are fundamental to entanglement distribution in quantum networks. However, they operate under a stringent operation reliability threshold and rely on resource-intensive recurrence purification rounds, each consuming two entangled pairs to probabilistically produce one. In the literature, hyperentanglement has been proposed to exploit multiple degrees of freedom (DOF), such as polarisation and spatial modes, to encode independent entangled states within a single photon pair. This has led to the definition of DAEPP (DOF-Assisted Entanglement Purification Protocol), which we propose to integrate with the BDCZ scheme, resulting in a chain protocol that we call DAHR (DOF-Assisted Hyperentanglement Repeater). The DAEPP step distils the fidelity of a DOF by consuming other(s). In this work, we propose and analyse a DAHR variant which integrates DAEPP at every segment of an end-to-end path combined with BDCZ. We derive a closed-form end-to-end fidelity recursion that embeds single-segment DAEPP into the BDCZ scheme and give a strict resource lower bound for any BDCZ baseline utilising BBPSSW purification to match DAHR's per-segment effective fidelity. At a representative asymmetric operating point informed by prior experiment, we show numerically that matching DAHR's single-photon-pair performance requires two to three rounds of BBPSSW purification. Additionally, below a critical operation reliability, no amount of BBPSSW rounds matches DAHR's one DAEPP round performance.

</details>


### [104] [Metrological quantum-to-classical crossover in the volume of a noisy quasiperiodic lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30834)
*Priya Ghosh,Debarupa Saha,Ujjwal Sen,Debraj Rakshit*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于Aubry-André-Harper模型的局域化-非局域化转变量子传感器的计量学性能，发现噪声与系统尺寸的相互作用会导致量子-经典交叉现象，使得量子增强的传感优势仅在有限的系统尺寸内有效，超过临界尺寸后量子优势消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来提出利用局域化-非局域化转变构建高效量子多体临界传感器，但其在实际噪声环境下的计量学性能和可扩展性尚未得到系统研究。需要明确噪声如何影响量子Fisher信息的标度行为，以及量子增强传感的适用范围和限制条件。

Method: 采用Aubry-André-Harper模型作为研究对象，该模型在有限强度的在位势下支持局域化-非局域化转变。通过计算量子Fisher信息分析系统的计量学性能，系统研究了两种噪声机制的影响：(1)热噪声效应；(2)晶格跃迁强度的无序缺陷。分析了不同噪声强度和系统尺寸下量子Fisher信息的标度行为，识别量子-经典交叉点和特征交叉长度。

Result: 研究发现噪声与系统尺寸的相互作用导致计量学量子-经典交叉现象。对于热噪声：在局域化-非局域化转变点附近，小系统展现量子增强标度，但超过特征交叉长度后量子优势消失；交叉长度随温度升高而减小。对于跃迁强度缺陷：量子增强区域(超广延标度)在足够大的系统尺寸时转变为经典受限的广延标度。两种不同噪声机制表现出相似的交叉行为。

Conclusion: 不同噪声机制对量子增强传感的可扩展性存在共同限制：将探测器尺寸增大到超过噪声依赖的临界尺寸会破坏计量学量子优势。这一发现揭示了基于局域化-非局域化转变的量子传感器在实际应用中的根本性限制，即量子增强传感仅在有限系统尺寸范围内有效，"更大"并不总是"更好"。

Abstract: Localization-delocalization transitions have recently been proposed for building a class of efficient quantum many-body critical sensors. This work scrutinizes metrological performances of such devices by focusing on the Aubry-André-Harper model that supports a localization-delocalization transition at finite strength of the onsite potential. We identify a metrological quantum-to-classical crossover driven by the interplay between noise and system size, whereby quantum-enhanced scaling of the quantum Fisher information persists only up to a finite, noise-dependent characteristic system-size. We first consider thermal noise and show that, at and near the localization-delocalization transition, the quantum Fisher information exhibits quantum-enhanced scaling for small systems but the system is stripped of this advantage beyond the characteristic crossover length. The crossover length decreases with increasing temperature. We then consider imperfections in the lattice hopping strengths and find a qualitatively similar crossover. There, the quantum-enhanced regime, identified with super-extensive scaling, gives way to an extensive scaling-a classical-limited weaker form-at sufficiently large system sizes. Thus, distinct noise mechanisms lead to a common limitation on the scalability of quantum-enhanced sensing: increasing the probe size beyond a noise-dependent limit can destroy the metrological quantum advantage.

</details>


### [105] [Size-Independent Robustness in Multipartite Bell Self-Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30851)
*Shen Cao,Xingjian Zhang,Fei Shi,Qi Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种与量子网络规模无关的多体纠缠自测试方法,针对n量子比特GHZ态推导出完全解析的设备无关自测试界,该界与违反误差呈线性关系,且在最优理论上界的两倍常数因子内,使得在任意大规模系统中可在固定噪声水平下认证GHZ纠缠,实现可扩展的设备无关验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的实用鲁棒多体纠缠自测试方法仅限于小规模系统,因为误差界会随系统规模严重退化。为实现大规模量子网络的设备无关验证,迫切需要开发一种鲁棒性与系统规模无关的多体自测试方法,以便在任意大的系统中认证量子纠缠。

Method: 采用算子不等式框架,针对n量子比特GHZ态推导完全解析的设备无关自测试界。该界与观测到的违反误差呈线性比例关系,并且普遍位于理论上界的两倍常数因子内。通过将推测的最优界的验证简化为高效的数值检验,在n=100的规模下完成了验证。

Result: 成功建立了鲁棒性与量子网络规模无关的多体自测试方法。推导的自测试界具有线性扩展性,在最多100个量子比特的系统中通过数值验证了推测的最优界。证明了在固定噪声水平下可以在任意大规模系统中认证GHZ纠缠。

Conclusion: 本研究突破了多体纠缠自测试在规模上的限制,实现了与系统规模无关的鲁棒自测试。通过解析界和高效数值验证,证明GHZ纠缠可在任意大的量子系统中进行设备无关认证,为可扩展的量子网络验证奠定了理论基础,具有重要的实用价值。

Abstract: Practical robust self-testing of multipartite entanglement has so far been restricted to small-scale systems due to error bounds that degrade severely with system size. In this work, we establish multipartite self-testing with robustness independent of the size of the quantum network. We derive a fully analytic, device-independent self-testing bound for $n$-qubit Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger (GHZ) states. The bound scales linearly with the observed violation error and lies universally within a constant factor of two from a theoretical upper bound. Furthermore, the operator-inequality framework reduces the verification of the conjectured optimal bound to a highly efficient numerical check, which we perform up to $n=100$. Consequently, GHZ entanglement can be certified under a fixed noise level in arbitrarily large systems, enabling scalable device-independent verification.

</details>


### [106] [Well-conditioned iterative methods for large open quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30860)
*Gaspard Beugnot,Paul Gregory,Rémi Robin,Antoine Tilloy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效求解林德布拉德主方程（Lindblad Master Equation）的迭代方法，通过利用无跳跃演化算符的逆作为预条件子，将稳态求解和低激发态计算的复杂度从O(n^6)降低到每次迭代O(n^3)，在CPU和GPU上实现了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于大规模马尔可夫开放量子系统，传统线性代数方法求解林德布拉德主方程需要O(n^4)的存储空间和O(n^6)的对角化或求逆复杂度，计算代价极高。虽然算符应用只需O(n^3)，但直接使用迭代方法缺乏有效的预条件子而难以收敛。因此需要开发一种高效的预条件迭代方法来计算稳态、低激发态和演化轨迹。

Method: 核心方法是利用林德布拉德方程中无跳跃演化部分S可以被高效求逆这一关键观察。基于逆算符S^(-1)，构造了一个辅助的完全正迹保持（CPTP）映射Φ，其不动点直接关联到系统稳态ρ_∞，且其他特征值的模更小。使用该映射Φ进行迭代求解稳态，通过少量Arnoldi迭代即可收敛。对于低激发态问题，采用S^(-1)作为预条件子的位移-反演Arnoldi方法。此外，还构建了一个隐式时间积分器用于刚性系统的低精度演化计算。

Result: 该方法在稳态和低激发态求解问题上，每次迭代的复杂度为O(n^3)，在CPU和GPU上均达到了最先进的性能水平。对于刚性系统，所提出的隐式时间积分器在低精度范围内具有竞争力。算法通过少量Arnoldi迭代即可找到稳态解，显著提高了大规模量子系统的计算效率。

Conclusion: 通过巧妙利用林德布拉德方程中无跳跃演化算符的可逆性，本文成功开发了一套高效的预条件迭代算法框架，解决了大规模马尔可夫开放量子系统稳态、低激发态和演化轨迹的计算难题。该方法将计算复杂度从O(n^6)降低到每次迭代O(n^3)，为量子系统的设计和表征提供了实用的高性能计算工具，在现代计算平台上表现出色。

Abstract: Markovian open quantum systems are well modeled by the Lindblad Master Equation (ME) $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} ρ_t = \mathcal{L} ρ_t$, where $\mathcal{L}$ is a linear (super-)operator and $ρ_t$ is the system state, a positive matrix. When designing or characterizing a quantum system, one is usually interested in the steady state $ρ_\infty$ (such that $\mathcal{L} ρ_\infty = 0$), the first few excited states, and trajectories $t\mapsto ρ_t$. In finite dimension, $ρ_t$ is an $n\times n$ matrix, $\mathcal{L}$ thus typically costs $n^4$ to store explicitly as a dense matrix, and $O(n^6)$ to diagonalize or invert exactly, making standard linear algebraic techniques expensive for large systems. However, $\mathcal{L}$ usually costs only $O(n^3)$ to apply. This makes iterative methods appealing, but they do not work without a good preconditioner. In this article, our main observation is that a part of the Lindblad equation, corresponding to the so-called no-jump evolution $\mathcal{S}$, can be inverted efficiently. Using this inverse map, we introduce an auxiliary completely positive trace-preserving (CPTP) map $Φ$ whose fixed point is directly related to $ρ_\infty$, all the other eigenvalues having smaller magnitude. The map $Φ$ is thus well suited to iterative methods, and $ρ_\infty$ can be found in a few Arnoldi iterations. Using the same inverse map $\mathcal{S}^{-1}$ as preconditioner, we compute the low-lying spectrum efficiently via shift-invert Arnoldi, and, as a proof of concept, build an implicit time integrator that is competitive on stiff systems in the low-precision regime. For the steady-state and low excited states problems, our methods scale like $O(n^3)$ per iteration and offer state-of-the-art performance on CPU and GPU.

</details>


### [107] [Constrained minimax approximation for quantum signal processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30937)
*Yulong Dong,James B. Larsen,Lin Lin,Rahul Sarkar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子信号处理(QSP)中的约束极小极大逼近问题，提出了Remez交换法和非线性傅里叶回缩法两种方法来处理半无限约束的可行性问题，并在qsppack软件包中实现了完整的工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信号处理的经典设计阶段需要求解约束极小极大逼近问题：寻找具有特定奇偶性的多项式，使其在拟合集上一致逼近目标函数，同时在定义域[0,1]上的幅度保持不大于1（半无限约束）。传统的离散化方法将问题转换为线性规划，但在有限采样点上的可行性不能保证整个定义域上的可行性，特别是当最优逼近多项式达到可行集边界时，这一问题尤为突出。

Method: 提出两种解决方法：（1）结合主动集约束实施的Remez交换法，该方法在许多测试实例上高效，但其稳定性依赖于目标函数和问题几何特性；（2）非线性傅里叶回缩法，该方法利用QSP完备化和相位合成技术，将近似可行的多项式转化为可行QSP多项式的相位因子，且不增加多项式次数。回缩法在很大程度上保持逼近精度，并在Remez启发式方法不稳定的实例上仍然有效。

Result: 在具有代表性的测试问题中，非线性傅里叶回缩法能够基本保持逼近精度，并且在Remez启发式方法不稳定的情况下仍保持有效性。所提出的工作流程成功地将经典极小极大逼近和半无限优化与非线性傅里叶分析联系起来。

Conclusion: 本文构建了连接经典极小极大逼近、半无限优化与非线性傅里叶分析的完整工作流程，并在qsppack软件包中实现。非线性傅里叶回缩法为解决QSP中的半无限约束可行性问题提供了稳健的解决方案，相比Remez交换法具有更好的稳定性和适用性，为量子信号处理的实际应用提供了有效的计算工具。

Abstract: Quantum signal processing (QSP) provides a simple and efficient framework for implementing polynomial transformations using quantum circuits. Its classical design stage leads to a constrained minimax approximation problem: find a polynomial of prescribed parity that approximates a target function uniformly on a fitting set while remaining bounded in magnitude by one on the domain $[0,1]$, which can be viewed as a semi-infinite constraint. Discretization converts the problem into a linear program, but feasibility at a set of finitely many sampled points does not ensure feasibility on the whole domain, especially when an optimal approximant reaches the boundary of the feasible set. We investigate two approaches to address this difficulty. A Remez exchange method combined with active-set constraint enforcement is efficient on many tested instances, but its stability depends on the target and problem geometry. We then introduce nonlinear Fourier retraction, which uses QSP completion and phase synthesis to turn a nearly feasible polynomial into phase factors for a feasible QSP polynomial without increasing the degree. Across representative problems, retraction largely preserves approximation accuracy and remains effective on instances where the Remez heuristic is unstable. The resulting workflow connects classical minimax approximation and semi-infinite optimization with nonlinear Fourier analysis, and is implemented in the qsppack software package.

</details>


### [108] [Work Extraction Across a Thermodynamic Hierarchy in Quantum Many-Body Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31001)
*Akihiro Hokkyo,Masahito Ueda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了孤立量子多体系统中热力学的层级结构，表明同一量子态在不同操作层级下可以呈现热态或非热态特性。扩展允许操作可将隐藏的非热性转化为可提取功，其功增益由不同层级间的熵密度差（即互信息密度）所界定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热力学概念（如热平衡和可提取功）依赖于可观测量的选择和允许的操作。然而，孤立量子多体系统中是否存在依赖于操作层级的热力学结构，以及如何通过扩展操作来利用隐藏的非热性资源提取额外功，这些问题尚未被系统研究。作者旨在建立量子多体态的热力学层级理论框架。

Method: 作者构建了一个基于可观测量和允许操作的热力学层级结构模型。通过分析三种操作扩展方式——提高空间分辨率、延长控制时间和引入非局域连接性——建立了功增益与不同层级间熵密度差的定量关系。该熵密度差以互信息密度的形式表达，分别对应于位置-态Holevo信息、相邻区域间关联和空间非局域关联。利用信息论工具推导了各层级间功提取的理论上界。

Result: 研究确立了三种操作扩展情况下的功增益界限：(1)空间分辨率提升可获取位置-态Holevo信息；(2)控制时长增加可利用相邻区域间的关联；(3)非局域连接性可访问空间非局域关联。所有情况下，功增益均由相应层级间的熵密度差（互信息密度）界定。这表明量子多体系统中存在可操作的热力学层级，隐藏的非热性可通过扩展操作转化为可提取功资源。

Conclusion: 孤立量子多体态具有内在的热力学层级结构，同一态在不同操作层级下表现出不同的热力学性质。扩展允许操作能够将隐藏的非热性转化为可提取功，其上界由不同层级间的熵密度差（互信息密度形式）决定。这一框架为理解量子多体系统的热力学性质和优化量子功提取提供了新的理论基础，揭示了信息论量与热力学资源之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: Thermodynamics is operational in the sense that the very concept of thermal equilibrium depends crucially on the choice of observables, while the amount of extractable work is defined relative to the allowed operations. Here we show that isolated quantum many-body states admit a thermodynamic hierarchical structure of observables and allowed operations, where the same state can be thermal at one level of the hierarchy and athermal at another. Enlarging the set of allowed operations therefore renders such hidden athermality a potential resource for work extraction; the resulting work gain is bounded in terms of the difference between the entropy densities of the two levels. This entropy difference takes the form of the mutual-information density. We establish the bounds for three extensions of operational access: increased spatial resolution, increased duration of control, and nonlocal connectivity, which provide access to position-state Holevo information, correlations between neighboring regions and spatially nonlocal correlations, respectively. Thus the difference between entropies at different levels of the thermodynamic hierarchy governs the bound on the work gain associated with moving to a less restricted level.

</details>


### [109] [Protected Logical Qudits in Kitaev Quantum Double Models via Stable Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31011)
*Naihong Hu,Futao Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为有限群Kitaev量子双模型中的受保护逻辑量子态建立了表示论框架，通过引入ε-稳定不可约表示给出了存在性准则，并构造了任意维度的受保护逻辑量子态，最终实现了基于D(A_4)模型的通用逻辑三态量子计算方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算需要对编码的量子信息进行鲁棒保护。现有的Kitaev量子双模型缺乏系统的表示论框架来构造受保护的逻辑量子态（logical qudits），特别是对于一般有限群的情况，如何建立存在性准则和构造方法仍不明确。本文旨在发展一套完整的数学框架，系统地解决有限群量子双模型中受保护逻辑量子态的构造问题。

Method: 1. 引入ε-稳定不可约表示的概念作为理论核心工具；2. 利用表示论方法建立受保护逻辑量子态存在的充要条件准则；3. 推导带状算子-投影算子（ribbon-projector）的对易关系，证明其可产生d维受保护逻辑子空间；4. 将该框架应用于对称群Sn、交错群A4以及半直积群族(ℤ2)^d⋊ℤd，构造具体的逻辑量子比特和逻辑三态；5. 针对D(A4)模型，设计基于带状算子的逻辑门操作方案。

Result: 1. 成功为对称群Sn（n≥3）构造了受保护的逻辑量子比特；2. 为交错群A4构造了受保护的逻辑三态（qutrit）；3. 证明了半直积群族(ℤ2)^d⋊ℤd可以实现任意维度d≥2的受保护逻辑量子态；4. 对于D(A_4)量子双模型，完整描述了使用带状算子实现通用逻辑三态量子计算的具体方案；5. 建立的表示论框架具有普适性，可推广到其他有限群模型。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入ε-稳定不可约表示，为有限群Kitaev量子双模型中受保护逻辑量子态的构造建立了完整的表示论框架，给出了充要存在性准则和系统构造方法。研究证明了多个群族（对称群、交错群、特定半直积群）可支持不同维度的受保护逻辑量子态，并在D(A_4)模型上实现了通用逻辑三态量子计算方案。该框架为容错量子计算中的量子纠错码设计提供了新的数学工具和理论基础，对拓扑量子计算的发展具有重要意义。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computation requires robust protection of encoded quantum information. In this work, we develop a representation-theoretic framework for constructing protected logical qudits in finite-group Kitaev quantum double models. By introducing $\varepsilon$-stable irreducible representations, we establish a necessary and sufficient existence criterion and derive ribbon--projector commutation relations that yield a $d$-dimensional protected logical subspace. We apply this construction to symmetric and alternating groups, obtaining logical qubits for $S_n$ ($n\ge 3$) and a logical qutrit for $A_4$. Moreover, the family $(\mathbb Z_2)^d\rtimes\mathbb Z_d$ realizes protected logical qudits of arbitrary dimension $d\ge 2$. Finally, for $D(A_4)$, we describe a scheme for universal logical qutrit computation using ribbon-based logical operations.

</details>


### [110] [Entanglement and magic transitions in an all-to-all non-Hermitian spin model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31064)
*Sasanka Dowarah,Michael Kolodrubetz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个具有精确k-局域全对全相互作用的非厄米自旋系综模型,可以解析追踪从清洁极限到无序态的主导本征值演化。研究发现在特定无序类型下,系统会发生从低纠缠/低魔性的孤立态到高纠缠/高魔性的体态的转变,并将此机制映射到测量诱导的量子相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米多体相互作用系统的长时演化由虚部最大的本征值决定,但在强相互作用系统中这一本征值通常无法解析追踪,其选择的态的性质也未知。为了理解无序如何影响非厄米系统中主导本征态的性质(纠缠和魔性),以及孤立谱外点与多体谱体边缘之间的竞争机制,需要构建可解析处理的模型。

Method: 构建了一个包含L个自旋、具有精确k-局域全对全相互作用的非厄米自旋系综模型,在该模型中可以解析追踪主导本征值。系统研究了三种无序类型:纯反厄米无序、纯厄米无序和等强度混合无序。通过解析和数值方法分析了不同参数区间(k≫√L和k≪√L)下孤立态与体态的竞争,计算了纠缠熵和魔性(非稳定性)等量子信息量。最后将非厄米演化精确映射到受监测量子系统的后选择轨迹上。

Result: 对于纯反厄米无序和混合无序,当体态在虚部上超越孤立态时,主导本征态从低纠缠、低魔性的孤立态转变为高纠缠、高魔性的体态,纠缠和魔性在同一阈值处同步变化。在k≫√L时,体态具有尖锐的谱边缘,转变阈值可以闭合形式给出;在k≪√L时,谱尾展宽使相变变为交叉渡越。纯厄米无序则不存在孤立态到体态的转换,形成对比。成功建立了与测量诱导相变的联系。

Conclusion: 本文通过构建可解析的非厄米多体自旋模型,揭示了无序驱动的谱外点-体态竞争机制,证明了主导本征态的转变伴随纠缠和魔性的同步跃变。不同无序类型(反厄米、厄米、混合)展现出定性不同的行为,相变的清晰程度由k与√L的相对大小决定。通过与受监测量子系统的精确映射,为理解测量诱导相变提供了新的谱分析视角,连接了非厄米量子动力学与量子信息理论。

Abstract: The long-time state of a non-Hermitian system is determined by the eigenvalue with the largest imaginary part. In interacting many-body systems this eigenvalue usually cannot be tracked analytically, and the character of the state it selects is unknown. We construct a non-Hermitian spin ensemble of $L$ spins with exactly $k$-local all-to-all interactions, in which this dominant eigenvalue can be tracked analytically from the clean limit into the disordered regime. Disorder produces a competition between an isolated spectral outlier and the edge of a many-body spectral bulk. We study three cases: purely anti-Hermitian disorder, purely Hermitian disorder, and mixed disorder of equal strength. For purely anti-Hermitian and mixed disorder, we show that when the bulk overtakes the outlier in imaginary part, the dominant eigenstate switches from an outlier state with low entanglement and magic (nonstabilizerness) to a bulk state with substantially larger entanglement and magic, with both changing at the same threshold. For $k \gg \sqrt{L}$ the bulk has a sharp spectral edge and the transition thresholds follow in closed form, while for $k \ll \sqrt{L}$ spectral tails broaden the transition into a crossover. Purely Hermitian disorder provides a contrasting case with no outlier-to-bulk switching. Finally, we map the non-Hermitian evolution exactly onto postselected trajectories of a monitored quantum system, connecting this spectral mechanism to measurement-induced transitions.

</details>


### [111] [Deterministic Minimum-Output-Entropy Nonadditivity via Haagerup's Inequality and Near-Free Permutation Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31081)
*Guocheng Zhen,Chengkai Zhu,Ranyiliu Chen,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了Collins混合酉证明中最小输出熵非可加性的有限维二次证书的确定性实现，通过结合Haagerup长度二不等式和O'Donnell-Wu同时确定性谱逼近方法，构造了一个确定性多项式时间算法，实现了最小输出熵的严格非可加性界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息理论中的最小输出熵非可加性问题长期依赖于随机化构造方法，缺乏确定性实现。本文旨在为Collins的混合酉证明提供确定性的有限维二次证书，从而给出最小输出熵非可加性的确定性多项式时间算法构造，并深入分析其几何结构和逼近精度的渐近最优性。

Method: 本文采用组合方法，将Haagerup的长度二不等式与O'Donnell和Wu的同时确定性谱逼近技术相结合。对于固定整数K≥2和满足log K>2(3+η)²的有理数η>0，构造确定性多项式时间算法输出K个置换，通过限制其置换矩阵到非平凡标准表示获得实正交Stinespring块和量子信道。进一步证明常数3在相关厄米零对角系数类上渐近最优，分析有限谱转移的饱和度，并通过标准协变扩展将熵间隙转化为单次Holevo量的自张量超可加性。

Result: 算法对于足够大的目标尺寸N，输出作用在N'=N+o_{K,η}(N)个点上的K个置换，构造的量子信道Φ_{N'}满足最小输出熵的严格非可加性不等式：2H_min(Φ_{N'}) - H_min(Φ_{N'}^⊗2) ≥ log K/K - 2log(1+(3+η)²/K) > 0。证明了常数3在相关系数类上是渐近最优的，有限谱转移几乎达到饱和，并实现了单次Holevo量的自张量超可加性的确定性构造。

Conclusion: 本文成功给出了最小输出熵非可加性的首个确定性多项式时间构造，将Collins混合酉证明从随机化方法提升到确定性算法层面。通过证明常数3的渐近最优性和谱转移的近饱和性，揭示了完整输出体的精细几何结构是超越标量半径方法的自然下一层次改进方向，为量子信息理论中的可加性问题研究提供了新的确定性工具和几何洞察。

Abstract: We give a deterministic realization of the finite-dimensional quadratic certificate underlying Collins's mixed-unitary proof of minimum-output-entropy nonadditivity. For every fixed integer $K\ge 2$ and rational $η>0$ satisfying $\log K>2(3+η)^2$, a deterministic polynomial-time algorithm, for every sufficiently large target size $N$, outputs $K$ permutations on $N'=N+o_{K,η}(N)$ points. Restricting their permutation matrices to the nontrivial standard representation yields real orthogonal Stinespring blocks and a channel $Φ_{N'}:M_{N'-1}(\mathbb{C})\to M_K(\mathbb{C})$ such that \[ 2H_{\min}(Φ_{N'}) -H_{\min}(Φ_{N'}^{\otimes 2}) \ge \frac{\log K}{K} -2\log\left(1+\frac{(3+η)^2}{K}\right) >0. \] The construction combines Haagerup's length-two inequality with the simultaneous deterministic spectral approximation of O'Donnell and Wu. We further show that the constant $3$ is asymptotically sharp on the relevant Hermitian zero-diagonal coefficient class and that the finite spectral transfer is nearly saturated, thereby isolating the finer geometry of the full output body as the natural next level of refinement beyond the scalar-radius method. Finally, a standard covariant extension converts the same deterministic entropy gap exactly into self-tensor superadditivity of the one-shot Holevo quantity.

</details>


### [112] [Phase-noise induced many-body interference suppression in Gaussian Boson Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31089)
*Dario Cilluffo,Matthias Kost,Martin B. Plenio*

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于海森堡图像的张量网络方法来描述无碰撞高斯玻色采样(GBS),建立了输出概率的Fock空间表示,揭示了GBS概率的Hafnian结构以及相位不敏感贡献与干涉扇区的分解,并应用该框架研究了相位扩散对多体量子干涉的抑制效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯玻色采样实验中存在相位涨落等噪声因素,但缺乏清晰透明的理论框架来直接连接实验可测量的相位涨落与量子干涉损失之间的关系。现有的GBS理论描述难以直观揭示输出概率的内部结构以及干涉效应的层次性,需要发展一种更直接、更易于与实验对接的数学表述方法。

Method: 采用海森堡图像的张量网络形式化方法构建无碰撞高斯玻色采样的理论框架。该方法用实验可直接测量的物理量来表达输出概率的Fock空间形式,自然地恢复了Hafnian数学结构,并将GBS概率分解为相位不敏感部分和与完美匹配对相关联的干涉扇区层次结构。利用该框架分析相位扩散过程,研究其对多体量子干涉的逐步抑制效应。

Result: 成功建立了输出概率的直接Fock空间表达式,揭示了GBS概率的内在分解结构:相位不敏感贡献和分层的干涉扇区。研究发现相位扩散会逐步抑制多体量子干涉效应,使输出统计逐渐趋向经典的二聚体模型(dimer-model)行为。该框架能够定量地将实验表征的相位涨落与光子量子采样实验中量子干涉的损失直接关联起来。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了一个透明清晰的理论框架,通过海森堡图像张量网络方法成功连接了实验可测的相位涨落与光子量子采样中量子干涉损失。该框架不仅揭示了GBS概率的深层数学结构(ハfnian及干涉层次),还为理解和量化相位扩散等退相干机制对量子优势的影响提供了重要工具,对光子量子计算实验的噪声分析和性能评估具有实际指导意义。

Abstract: We develop a Heisenberg-picture tensor-network formulation of collision-free Gaussian Boson Sampling, providing a direct Fock-space expression for output probabilities in terms of experimentally accessible quantities. The resulting representation naturally recovers the Hafnian structure while revealing the decomposition of GBS probability into a phase-insensitive contribution and a hierarchy of interference sectors associated with pairs of perfect matchings. As an application, we investigate phase diffusion and show how it progressively suppresses many-body interference, driving the output statistics toward a classical dimer-model regime. Our results establish a transparent framework for connecting experimentally characterized phase fluctuations with the loss of quantum interference in photonic quantum sampling experiments.

</details>


### [113] [Tunable Exceptional Points for Quantum Sensing in a Spin--Orbit-Angular-Momentum Coupled BEC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31095)
*Zicheng Zhang,Xi-Wang Luo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于自旋-轨道-角动量耦合玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体的可调谐例外点(EP)量子传感平台,通过Bogoliubov动力学实现了内禀非厄米特性,避免了传统非厄米系统中的增益-损耗噪声问题,在宇称-时间对称稳定区域接近EP时实现了量子Fisher信息的显著增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非厄米系统中的例外点虽然对微弱扰动具有强放大响应,但其增强的光谱灵敏度因增益和损耗噪声而无法有效转化为量子极限传感性能的提升。因此,需要寻找一种基于厄米微观哈密顿量的内禀非厄米动力学系统,以实现真正的量子增强传感。

Method: 从完全厄米的微观哈密顿量出发,推导出具有宇称-时间(PT)对称性的Bogoliubov动力学矩阵;通过Raman耦合和相互作用参数调控例外点;识别多个例外点并绘制其轨迹和稳定性图景;计算量子Fisher信息以评估传感性能;分析二阶EP附近的能隙打开速率作为传感性能指标;研究两个二阶EP重叠时的协同增强效应;提出自旋密度测量方案逼近量子Fisher信息极限。

Result: 在从PT对称稳定区域接近例外点时,量子Fisher信息显著增强;二阶EP附近的能隙打开速率可作为比较不同EP传感性能的相对指标;高阶EP不一定能从稳定区域访问;两个二阶EP调谐至重叠时,其传感贡献叠加,总量子Fisher信息呈线性增强;自旋密度测量可以接近量子Fisher信息极限。

Conclusion: 本研究基于自旋-轨道-角动量耦合玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体的内禀Bogoliubov非厄米动力学,成功构建了可调谐的例外点量子传感平台,克服了传统非厄米系统的噪声限制,实现了量子Fisher信息的显著增强,并提供了实验可行的自旋密度测量方案,为玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体中可调谐EP增强量子传感开辟了实验路径。

Abstract: Exceptional points (EPs) can induce strongly amplified responses to weak perturbations, but enhanced spectral sensitivity in conventional non-Hermitian systems does not necessarily translate into improved quantum-limited sensing because of the associated gain and loss noise. Here, we investigate a tunable EP sensing platform based on the intrinsic non-Hermitian Bogoliubov dynamics of a spin-orbit-angular-momentum-coupled Bose-Einstein condensate. Starting from a fully Hermitian microscopic Hamiltonian, we derive a Bogoliubov dynamical matrix that exhibits parity-time (PT) symmetry, with EPs tunable through the Raman coupling and interaction parameters. We identify multiple EPs and map their trajectories and associated stability landscapes, revealing strong quantum Fisher information enhancement when the EPs are approached from the PT-symmetric stable regime. We further find that the gap-opening rate around a second-order EP provides a useful relative indicator for comparing the sensing performance of EPs, while higher-order EPs are not necessarily accessible from the stable regime. Moreover, two second-order EPs can be tuned to overlap, allowing their sensing contributions to add and yielding a linear enhancement of the total quantum Fisher information. Finally, we show that a spin-density measurement can approach the quantum Fisher information limit, providing an experimentally accessible route to tunable EP-enhanced quantum sensing in Bose-Einstein condensates.

</details>


### [114] [Unconditional Certified Randomness without Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31112)
*Andrea Coladangelo,Dakshita Khurana,Saachi Mutreja,Bhaskar Roberts,Joseph Slote,Avishay Tal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子随机预言机模型中提出了一个经过认证的随机性协议，该协议具有非交互性和公开可验证性，基于Yamakawa和Zhandry的量子性证明，并证明了对亚指数级自适应量子查询攻击的无条件安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于随机预言机下的认证随机性工作需要额外假设Aaronson-Ambainis猜想，或仅能证明对低查询深度对手的安全性。本研究旨在在量子随机预言机模型下，无需额外假设即可实现对更强大对手（亚指数级自适应量子查询）的无条件安全性证明。

Method: 该协议基于Yamakawa和Zhandry的量子性证明框架构建，采用非交互式设计，使用经典验证器进行公开验证。在量子随机预言机模型中进行安全性分析，针对能够进行亚指数级数量自适应量子查询的对手建立无条件安全性证明。

Result: 成功构建了一个在量子随机预言机模型中的认证随机性协议，该协议实现了非交互性和公开可验证性特征，使用经典验证器即可完成验证。证明了该协议对执行亚指数级自适应量子查询的对手具有无条件安全性，这在查询复杂度上优于之前的工作。

Conclusion: 本文提出的认证随机性协议在量子随机预言机模型下取得了重要进展，无需依赖Aaronson-Ambainis猜想等额外假设，即可实现对亚指数级自适应量子查询对手的无条件安全性。该协议的非交互性、公开可验证性和经典验证器设计使其在实用性上具有优势，为量子密码学中的认证随机性生成提供了更强的安全保证。

Abstract: We obtain a certified randomness protocol in the quantum random oracle model. The protocol is non-interactive and publicly verifiable with a classical verifier, and is based on Yamakawa and Zhandry's proof of quantumness [JACM'24]. We prove unconditional security of this protocol against adversaries making subexponentially-many adaptive quantum queries to the random oracle.
  Prior work on certified randomness relative to a random oracle additionally assumed the Aaronson--Ambainis conjecture or proved security only against low query-depth adversaries.

</details>


### [115] ["Train classical, deploy quantum" requires rethinking generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31117)
*Snehal Raj,Natansh Mathur,Alejandro Perdomo-Ortiz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子生成模型的泛化能力，发现使用矩匹配损失（如MMD²）训练的模型在泛化性能上劣于似然训练的模型，表明收敛的矩匹配损失并非泛化能力的可靠指标，"经典训练-量子部署"工作流需要直接针对泛化的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子生成模型被视为量子计算最有前景的应用之一，目前主流策略是在经典计算机上训练模型并在量子设备上生成样本。然而，使用可在经典计算机上评估的训练损失（如矩匹配损失MMD²）训练的模型，其泛化能力（而非仅复制训练统计量）仍未被充分理解，需要系统评估这类模型是否能产生真正泛化的生成模型。

Method: 通过直接采样对多种量子和经典生成模型进行基准测试，比较矩匹配损失训练和似然训练模型的泛化性能。在两个应用驱动的数据集上进行实验：（1）最多30量子比特的基数约束数据集；（2）基因组单核苷酸变异数据集（有效集即为观测数据）。评估模型在训练集外数据上的表现，以此衡量泛化能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，使用矩匹配损失训练的模型普遍比似然训练的模型表现出更差的泛化性能。在两个数据集上的基准测试均验证了这一发现，说明收敛的矩匹配损失不能作为模型泛化能力的可靠衡量标准。

Conclusion: 收敛的矩匹配损失不是泛化能力的可靠度量指标。"经典训练-量子部署"工作流需要直接针对泛化能力的新方法。这留下了一个开放性问题：是改进训练目标函数就足够，还是必须改变模型架构本身。研究结果对量子生成模型的实际应用具有重要指导意义，指出当前训练范式的局限性。

Abstract: Generative models have become central across science and industry, from image and text synthesis to the design of molecules and materials. Quantum generative models are considered one of the most promising applications for quantum computers, since a quantum circuit naturally produces samples from the distribution it encodes, and for suitable circuits that distribution is believed to be hard for any classical computer to reproduce. A leading strategy trains these models on a classical computer and reserves the quantum device for generating samples at deployment. This is possible when the training loss can be evaluated on a classical computer. A prime example is the maximum mean discrepancy (MMD$^2$), a moment-matching loss that compares the model and the data through their Pauli-$Z$ correlations. Research so far has asked whether such models can be trained and whether their sampling is hard; whether minimizing such an objective yields a model that generalizes, rather than one that merely reproduces the training statistics, remains poorly understood. We benchmark a broad set of quantum and classical generative models by direct sampling and show that models trained with a moment-matching loss generally show worse generalization than the likelihood-trained models. We show this on two application-inspired datasets: first a cardinality-constrained dataset at up to $30$ qubits and second a dataset of genomic single-nucleotide variants, whose valid set is the observed data. These results indicate that a converged moment-matching loss is not a reliable measure of generalization, and that train-classical, deploy-quantum workflows will need approaches that target generalization directly, leaving open whether better training objectives suffice or whether the model architectures themselves must change.

</details>


### [116] [Engineering multi-photon dissipation with a dc-voltage-biased Josephson junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31154)
*Marco Paradina,Ambroise Peugeot,Roberto Negrin,Oscar Novat,Tristan Villain,Anil Murani,Jean-Loup Ville,Sébastien Jezouin,Raphaël Lescanne,Audrey Bienfait,Benjamin Huard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出使用直流电压偏置的SQUID来工程化实现多光子耗散，避免了传统参数泵浦方法中的寄生非线性项，并通过Wigner层析成像验证了双光子耗散过程，为玻色子量子比特的储库工程和cat-qubit稳定化提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的多光子耗散（玻色子量子比特的关键资源）通常通过参数泵浦Josephson耦合器实现，但会产生有害的寄生非线性项。需要开发一种新方法来工程化多光子耗散，同时消除这些寄生效应。

Method: 使用直流电压偏置的SQUID（超导量子干涉器件）来工程化实现多光子耗散。该方法利用直流偏置使寄生非线性项平均抵消，激活高Q模式的一个、两个或四个光子转换为损耗模式的单个光子的过程。通过Wigner层析成像技术对双光子耗散过程进行表征和验证。

Result: 成功实现了单光子、双光子和四光子到单光子的转换过程。通过Wigner层析成像对双光子耗散进行了详细表征，证实了直流偏置结作为储库工程资源的有效性。实验验证了该方法能够有效抑制传统参数泵浦方法中存在的寄生非线性项。

Conclusion: 直流电压偏置的SQUID为储库工程提供了一种有效的新资源，是实现cat-qubit稳定化的可行路径。该方法通过消除寄生非线性项，为玻色子量子比特的多光子耗散工程化提供了更优的解决方案，在量子信息处理和量子纠错领域具有重要应用前景。

Abstract: Multi-photon dissipation -- a key resource for bosonic qubits -- is usually realized by parametrically pumping a Josephson coupler at the cost of spurious nonlinear terms. Here we instead engineer it using a dc-voltage-biased SQUID, such that these parasitic terms average out. We activate the conversion of one, two, or four photons of a high-Q mode into a single photon of a lossy mode. We characterize the two-photon dissipation by Wigner tomography, establishing dc-biased junctions as a resource for reservoir engineering and a viable route to cat-qubit stabilization.

</details>


### [117] [Bosonic codes from compact phase spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31156)
*David Roberts,Aaron Slipper,Alireza Parhizkar,Victor V. Albert,Mohammad Hafezi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了亏格二黎曼曲面上的玻色子量子纠错码的代数结构，通过自守形式构造码字并生成完整的码空间塔。证明了一个基本的不可行定理：对于任何亏格大于一的情况，稳定子群是非顺从的，导致稳定子哈密顿量中存在严格正的谱隙，因此不存在可归一化的量子态能满足所有稳定子条件，这与标准GKP码形成鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP)码基于稳定子群$\mathbb{Z}^2$的顺从性，可以实现任意精度的近似码字。然而，将量子纠错码推广到更高亏格的黎曼曲面时，其代数结构和物理性质尚不清楚。本研究旨在探索亏格二黎曼曲面上玻色子量子纠错码的基本限制，理解稳定子群的代数性质如何影响量子纠错码的存在性和可实现性。

Method: 1. 利用自守形式理论显式构造亏格二黎曼曲面上的码字；2. 解析地生成所有权重下的完整码空间塔结构；3. 通过群论分析证明亏格大于一时稳定子群的非顺从性；4. 利用算子理论和谱分析方法证明稳定子哈密顿量具有严格正的谱隙；5. 从数学上严格论证不存在满足所有稳定子条件的可归一化量子态。

Result: 成功构造了亏格二黎曼曲面上玻色子量子纠错码的完整代数结构和码空间塔。证明了一个基本的不可行定理：当亏格大于一时，稳定子群必然是非顺从的，这导致稳定子哈密顿量中出现严格正的谱隙。该结果的直接推论是不存在可归一化的量子态能够同时满足所有稳定子条件，这与亏格为一（环面）情况下的GKP码存在根本性差异。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了高亏格黎曼曲面上玻色子量子纠错码的基本数学障碍。稳定子群的非顺从性是一个拓扑不变的性质，对于任何亏格大于一的情况都成立，这从根本上限制了此类量子纠错码的物理可实现性。该不可行定理表明，标准GKP码的构造方法无法直接推广到高亏格曲面，需要发展全新的理论框架来处理非顺从稳定子群的情况。这一结果对理解量子纠错码的拓扑和代数限制具有重要意义。

Abstract: We present the algebraic structure of bosonic quantum error-correcting codes on genus-two Riemann surfaces. We explicitly construct the code words as automorphic forms and analytically generate the full tower of code spaces at all weights. We prove a fundamental no-go theorem: for any genus greater than one, the stabilizer group is non-amenable, forcing a strictly positive spectral gap in the stabilizer Hamiltonian. Consequently, no normalizable quantum state can satisfy all stabilizer conditions. This sharply contrasts with standard Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) codes, where the amenability of the stabilizer group $\mathbb{Z}^2$ permits approximate code words with arbitrary precision.

</details>


### [118] [Efficient search for excitable zero-modes in constrained systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31165)
*Jean-Yves Desaules*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效协议,用于在动力学约束系统(如Rydberg原子阵列)中寻找平移不变可激发零模(EZMs)的解析表达式,基于局域非约束哈密顿量的特征分解。该方法在装饰Rydberg链模型中成功构造了连续的矩阵乘积态流形,覆盖零能量本征空间,并推导出非零动量零模和精确量子疤痕态的解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学约束系统(如阻塞区Rydberg原子阵列)因其能谱中存在非典型本征态而备受关注,包括量子多体疤痕态和异常大的零模子空间。其中一些可激发零模(EZMs)在开边界条件下可被提升至非零能量。然而,缺乏系统化的方法来解析地寻找这些平移不变的EZMs。本文旨在开发一种基于局域非约束哈密顿量特征分解的高效协议,以系统地找到约束系统中EZMs的解析表达式。

Method: 提出了一种基于局域非约束哈密顿量特征分解的高效协议来寻找平移不变可激发零模的解析表达式。该方法的核心是利用局域哈密顿量的本征基结构,系统地构造满足约束条件的零能量本征态。作者将该协议应用于装饰Rydberg链模型,通过分析局域本征基的物理结构,直接生成位于零能量本征空间的连续矩阵乘积态(MPS)流形。该方法还能推广到非零动量零模和精确疤痕态的构造。

Result: 在装饰Rydberg链模型上,该协议成功生成了完全位于周期边界条件下零能量本征空间的连续矩阵乘积态流形。该流形的张成空间随系统尺寸指数增长,对于奇数系统尺寸,它覆盖了整个零动量、零能量的本征空间。此外,基于流形的物理结构,作者推导出非零动量零模的解析表达式,以及在能量E=±√3处多项式数量的精确量子疤痕态的解析表达式。这些结果展示了该方法在解析构造特殊本征态方面的强大能力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于局域哈密顿量特征分解的协议为在动力学约束系统中系统地寻找平移不变可激发零模提供了高效途径。该方法不仅能够解析构造零能量本征态的连续流形,还能推广至非零动量零模和精确量子疤痕态的解析表达式。在装饰Rydberg链模型中的成功应用表明,该协议具有普适性,可为理解约束系统中的非典型本征态结构和纠缠特性提供重要工具,并可能推广到其他具有类似约束结构的量子多体系统。

Abstract: Kinetically constrained systems, such as those representing Rydberg atom arrays in the blockade regime, have gathered considerable attention due to the presence of atypical eigenstates in their spectrum. The latter manifests itself through the presence of quantum many-body scars as well as unusually large zero-mode (ZM) subspaces which contain analytically tractable eigenstates with various entanglement scalings. In particular, some of the latter are excitable zero-modes (EZMs), meaning that they can be promoted to a non-zero energy for open boundary conditions. In this work, I present an efficient protocol for finding analytical expressions for translation-invariant EZMs in constrained systems, based on the eigendecomposition of the local unconstrained Hamiltonian. I demonstrate the power of this method on a decorated Rydberg chain. In that model, my protocol directly produces a continuous matrix-product-state manifold located entirely in the zero-energy eigenspace for periodic boundary conditions. The span of that manifold grows exponentially with the system, and for odd system sizes it covers the entire zero-momentum eigenspace with zero energy. I then show how the physical structure of the manifold, which is tied to the local eigenbasis, also allows one to derive analytical expressions for ZMs at non-zero momentum and for a polynomial number of exact scars at $E=\pm\sqrt{3}$.

</details>

### [119] [Reimagining Gravity: Generalized Symmetries, Double Copy, and Spinning Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28736)
*Joon-Hwi Kim*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过三个不同视角研究广义相对论中的开放性问题:将引力视为洛伦兹群的规范理论以建立一形式对称性;探讨引力作为微分同胚规范理论与色-运动学对偶及双拷贝的关系;利用自对偶分解方法推导四维旋转黑洞的精确解及其点粒子有效场论动力学,并发展了手征形式体系来简化计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代物理学通过广义对称性、散射振幅和有效场论对广义相对论的探索提出了三个核心问题:1)动力学引力作为低能有效场论是否存在广义对称性?2)广义相对论与杨-米尔斯理论之间的树图双拷贝关系是否有场论解释?3)外场中四维旋转黑洞在点粒子有效理论框架下的精确运动方程或拉格朗日量是什么?这些问题促使需要重新评估引力、时空和自旋的传统观点。

Method: 论文采用三个不同视角研究引力:(1)将引力视为洛伦兹群的规范理论,建立动力学引力的一形式对称性;(2)研究引力作为微分同胚规范理论的程度,与色-运动学对偶和双拷贝相关联;(3)将四维引力视为自对偶和反自对偶部分之间的非线性相互作用,应用于推导四维旋转黑洞解及其点粒子有效理论动力学。通过经典双拷贝推导新的自对偶黑洞度规,并提出Newman-Janis算法的动力学探测对应版本,利用手征形式体系进行拉格朗日推导。

Result: 研究获得以下成果:1)从经典双拷贝推导出新的自对偶黑洞度规;2)证明Kerr度规代表一对自对偶和反自对偶Taub-NUT解,将Newman-Janis算法提升为严格推导;3)提出Newman-Janis算法的动力学探测对应版本;4)给出旋转黑洞康普顿振幅的拉格朗日推导,该振幅对所有螺旋度配置展现正确的因子化并消除虚假极点;5)发展了手征形式体系,充分利用自对偶扇区的简单性。

Conclusion: 论文通过三个互补视角成功解决了广义相对论中的核心问题:建立了动力学引力的广义对称性框架,深化了引力与规范理论的双拷贝关系理解,并为四维旋转黑洞提供了严格的数学推导和有效场论描述。特别是通过自对偶分解和手征形式体系,不仅将Newman-Janis算法从经验方法提升为严格理论,还为计算旋转黑洞散射振幅提供了系统方法,推进了引力理论的现代理解和计算技术。

Abstract: General relativity is a century-old subject. Yet modern explorations through generalized symmetries, scattering amplitudes, and effective field theory have raised open problems, motivating a reassessment of conventional views on gravitation, spacetime, and spin. First, do generalized symmetries exist in dynamical gravity as a low-energy EFT? Second, is there a field-theoretic explanation for the tree-level double copy relationship between general relativity and Yang-Mills theory? Third, what are the exact equations of motion or Lagrangians of four-dimensional spinning black holes in external fields, in point-particle effective theory? In this dissertation, three different perspectives on gravity are developed to shed light on each of these puzzles.
  In Part I, we view gravity as a gauge theory of Lorentz group to establish a one-form symmetry of dynamical gravity. In Part II, we investigate how far gravity can be viewed as a gauge theory of diffeomorphisms, in connection with color-kinematics duality and double copy. In Part III, we view four-dimensional gravity as a nonlinear interaction between self-dual and anti-self-dual parts. We apply this view to the derivation of four-dimensional spinning black hole solutions as well as their dynamics in point-particle effective theory. First, we derive a new self-dual black hole metric from classical double copy and show that the Kerr metric represents a pair of self-dual and anti-self-dual Taub-NUT solutions, elevating the Newman-Janis algorithm to a rigorous derivation. Second, we propose a dynamical probe counterpart of Newman-Janis algorithm. We give a Lagrangian derivation of spinning black hole Compton amplitudes that exhibit correct factorizations and are free of spurious poles for all helicity configurations while developing a chiral formalism that manifests and maximally utilizes the simplicity of the self-dual sector.

</details>


### [120] [Carroll-Cotton Tensors and Gravitational Radiation at Null Infinity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28741)
*Adrien Fiorucci,Simon Pekar,P. Marios Petropoulos,Matthieu Vilatte*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过规范化共形Carroll代数，首次从第一性原理定义了共形Carroll几何上的Cotton张量，为描述四维渐近平坦时空共形边界上的引力辐射自由度提供了几何工具


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在描述引力波辐射时，传统的Bondi news张量需要补充数据（Geroch张量）才能获得完全协变的定义。需要寻找更自然的几何对象来量化引力波发射导致的非稳态偏离，同时在数学上需要量化Carrollian几何中偏离共形平坦的程度

Method: 采用规范场论方法，对共形Carroll代数进行规范化（gauging），从第一性原理出发构造Cotton张量。通过这种代数规范化过程，在共形Carroll几何框架下系统地导出具有共形协变性的Cotton张量

Result: 成功定义了共形Carroll几何上的Cotton张量，该张量具有共形协变性。数学上，它能够量化Carrollian几何偏离共形平坦的程度；物理上，它为四维渐近平坦时空的共形边界上的引力辐射自由度提供了几何描述工具。与需要补充Geroch张量数据的Bondi news相比，Carroll-Cotton张量提供了更自然的几何对象来量化引力波发射引起的非稳态性偏离

Conclusion: Carroll-Cotton张量是描述共形Carroll几何和引力辐射的自然且强大的几何工具。它克服了Bondi news张量需要补充数据的缺陷，为量化引力波辐射导致的非稳态性提供了完整的共形协变几何框架，在渐近平坦时空的引力辐射研究中具有重要应用价值

Abstract: We provide a first-principles definition of Cotton tensors on conformal Carroll geometries by gauging the conformal Carroll algebra. On the mathematical side, this analysis yields a conformally covariant object designed to quantify deviations from conformal flatness in Carrollian geometries. On the physical side, it offers a powerful geometric tool to describe gravitational radiative degrees of freedom at the conformal boundary of four-dimensional asymptotically flat spacetimes. Unlike the Bondi news, whose fully-covariant definition requires amendment by supplementary data that we identify with the Geroch tensor, the Carroll-Cotton tensors offer the natural geometric objects to quantify deviations from stationarity due to the emission of gravitational waves.

</details>


### [121] [OPE and correlation functions in a generally covariant form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28743)
*Arpit Das,Anatoly Konechny,Naveen Balaji Umasankar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究D≥3维弯曲空间上欧几里得共形场论的一般性质,包括共形平坦空间上标量主场的算子乘积展开(OPE)构造,以及非共形平坦空间上三点函数的短距离行为分析,发现需要对现有理论框架添加额外修正项以确保局域协变OPE的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于弯曲空间上共形场论的研究主要集中在平坦或共形平坦空间,对于更一般的非共形平坦弯曲空间上的共形场论性质(特别是OPE结构和三点函数行为)仍缺乏系统理解。已有的理论框架(如Parisini等人提出的ansatz)在处理非共形平坦空间时可能存在不完备性,需要进一步发展和修正。

Method: 1) 对于共形平坦空间:扩展前人工作,给出标量通道中descendant场对OPE贡献的一般性构造方法;2) 对于非共形平坦空间:采用环境空间形式体系(ambient space formalism),利用Parisini、Skenderis和Withers提出的一般ansatz来研究三点函数的短距离行为;3) 通过分析该ansatz的适用性,识别其局限性并提出必要的修正项以保证局域协变OPE的存在。

Result: 成功构造了共形平坦空间上标量通道中descendant场对OPE的一般性贡献;通过分析非共形平坦空间上三点函数的短距离行为,发现Parisini等人提出的ansatz需要添加额外的修正项;明确了保证局域协变OPE存在所需的修正条件。

Conclusion: 本文为理解高维弯曲空间上的共形场论提供了更完整的理论框架。对于共形平坦空间,给出了OPE中descendant贡献的系统构造;对于非共形平坦空间,指出了现有理论ansatz的不足,并通过引入必要的修正项完善了局域协变OPE的理论基础,这对于在一般弯曲背景下研究共形场论具有重要意义。

Abstract: In this paper we consider some general aspects of Euclidean conformal field theories on curved spaces of dimension $D\ge 3$. We first look at the OPE of scalar primary fields on conformally flat spaces. Extending the results of \cite{Konechny:2026bqg}, we give a general construction of the descendants' contributions to the OPE in the scalar channel. We then discuss CFTs on non-conformally flat spaces in the ambient space formalism. We investigate the short-distance behaviour of three-point functions on such spaces using the general ansatz proposed by Parisini, Skenderis, and Withers \cite{Parisini:2022wkb, Parisini:2023nbd}. We find that some additional corrections need to be added to the ansatz to ensure the existence of a local covariant OPE.

</details>


### [122] [Holographic warped CFTs from General Relativity on AdS$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28751)
*Kristiansen Lara,Ricardo Troncoso*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文为三维负宇宙常数爱因斯坦引力引入了一组新的边界条件,其正则生成元直接实现Virasoro代数与u(1) Kac-Moody代数的半直和,无需额外重定义整体荷。通过要求欧几里得BTZ黑洞与热AdS₃微分同胚,精确导出化学势的warped模形式S变换,相应的warped Cardy公式准确重现Bekenstein-Hawking熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边界条件在实现对称代数时需要额外的整体荷重定义,缺乏直接性和简洁性。本研究旨在构建一组新的边界条件,使正则生成元能够以标准形式直接实现Virasoro代数与u(1) Kac-Moody代数的半直和,同时正确描述三维AdS引力的热力学性质和微观态计数。

Method: 1) 为三维负宇宙常数爱因斯坦引力建立新的边界条件;2) 构造正则生成元并验证其直接实现Virasoro⊕u(1) Kac-Moody代数的半直和;3) 要求欧几里得BTZ黑洞与热AdS₃空间微分同胚,推导化学势的warped模形式S变换;4) 应用warped Cardy公式计算微观态熵,并与Bekenstein-Hawking熵比较;5) 将新边界条件与Brown-Henneaux及Compere-Song-Strominger边界条件进行显式对比分析。

Result: 正则生成元以标准形式直接实现了Virasoro代数与u(1) Kac-Moody代数的半直和,无需对整体荷进行额外重定义。通过微分同胚要求精确得到了化学势的warped模形式S变换。warped Cardy公式准确重现了BTZ黑洞的Bekenstein-Hawking熵,基态被正确识别为欧几里得AdS₃空间。完成了与已有Brown-Henneaux和Compere-Song-Strominger边界条件的显式比较。

Conclusion: 本文成功构建了三维AdS引力的新边界条件,其关键优势在于正则生成元直接、简洁地实现对称代数结构。通过微分同胚一致性条件自然导出warped模变换,并利用warped Cardy公式精确计算黑洞熵,为AdS₃/CFT₂对偶中的warped共形场论提供了更清晰的引力对偶描述框架。该边界条件与已有方案的对比进一步揭示了不同边界条件选择的物理等价性和技术差异。

Abstract: We introduce a new set of boundary conditions for three-dimensional Einstein gravity with negative cosmological constant. The canonical generators plainly realize the semidirect sum of Virasoro and $u(1)$ Kac-Moody algebras in its standard form, without the need of additional redefinitions of the global charges. We show that requiring Euclidean BTZ and thermal AdS$_3$ to be diffeomorphic precisely induces the warped modular $S$-transformation of the chemical potentials. The corresponding warped Cardy formula then exactly reproduces the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy, where the ground state is identified as Euclidean AdS$_3$. An explicit comparison with the boundary conditions of Brown-Henneaux and Compere-Song-Strominger is also addressed.

</details>


### [123] [Action-angle variables and phase space formulation of Hermitian matrix models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28942)
*Arghya Chattopadhyay*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从正交多项式递推关系出发，为大N厄米单矩阵模型建立了相空间描述，通过递推格点构造了半经典作用-角变量对，并将该框架推广至对称四次双割相和多割相情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大N矩阵模型在理论物理中具有重要地位，但其相空间结构的直接描述尚不完善。本文旨在从正交多项式递推关系这一基础数学结构出发，系统地构建矩阵模型的相空间描述，并揭示不同数学工具（递推格点、Wigner变换、平面谱曲线）之间的内在联系。

Method: 1. 单割相：从正交多项式满足的递推关系出发，利用递推格点自然构造半经典作用-角变量对，并将其正则映射到本征值和动量变量；通过正交多项式零点密度确定动量分布；验证该相空间结构与Christoffel-Darboux投影算子的Wigner变换及平面谱曲线的结果一致；2. 双割相：针对对称四次势，分析周期为2的Jacobi递推关系，构造两个Bloch能带和两个不连通的相空间分量，其作用量由部分't Hooft耦合常数给出；3. 多割推广：概述适用于多割相的有限间隙推广方案。

Result: 成功建立了从递推格点到相空间的映射关系，证明动量分布由正交多项式零点密度决定；验证了高斯模型的一致性；在对称四次双割相中，明确了两个Bloch能带对应的相空间结构和作用量与部分't Hooft耦合的关系；给出了多割情况的推广框架。

Conclusion: 正交多项式递推关系为大N矩阵模型提供了自然且系统的相空间描述途径。该方法不仅在单割相中与现有理论框架（Wigner变换、平面谱曲线）完全一致，还可有效推广至双割和多割复杂相结构，为理解矩阵模型的半经典几何结构提供了统一的数学框架。

Abstract: We develop a phase space description of large $N$ Hermitian one matrix models directly from the recursions satisfied by the orthogonal polynomials. In the one-cut phase, the recursion lattice naturally gives rise to a semiclassical action-angle pair, which can be mapped canonically to eigenvalue and momentum variables. We show that the resulting momentum profile is determined by the density of zeros of the orthogonal polynomials, and that the same phase space structure is reproduced from the Wigner transform of the Christoffel-Darboux projector and from the planar spectral curve. The Gaussian model provides a simple consistency check of the proposal. We then extend the construction to the symmetric quartic two-cut phase, where a period-two Jacobi recursion produces two Bloch bands and two disconnected phase space components with actions given by the partial 't Hooft couplings. Finally, we outline the finite-gap generalization appropriate to multicut phases.

</details>


### [124] [Flat connections on moduli spaces I: Local (1,0)-extension of the DHS connection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29169)
*Eric D'Hoker,Benjamin Enriquez,Oliver Schlotterer,Federico Zerbini*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文是系列工作的第一篇,旨在将DHS平坦联络从紧黎曼曲面上n点的配置空间扩展到Teichmüller空间。文章局部地显式求解了联络中对应于全纯方向的分量所满足的方程组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的平坦DHS联络$\mathcal J_{\mathrm{DHS}}$仅定义在固定紧黎曼曲面的配置空间上,具有模群不变性。为了更全面地理解其几何结构,需要将该联络扩展到更大的Teichmüller空间$\mathcal T_{h,n}$上,这要求联络取值于$\hat{\mathfrak t}_{h,n}$的导子李代数中。这一扩展是理解高亏格曲面上共形场论和可积系统深层联系的关键步骤。

Method: 选择适配于映射$\mathcal T_{h,n}\to \mathcal T_h$的局部坐标系,将扩展后的联络分解为三个部分:原有的$\mathcal J_{\mathrm{DHS}}$、对应于$\mathcal T_h$全纯方向的分量$\mathcal L$以及对应于反全纯方向的第三个分量。针对全纯方向分量$\mathcal L$,推导出其所满足的方程组系统,并在局部显式地求解该方程组,得到$\mathcal L$的具体表达式。

Result: 成功分离并显式求解了联络中全纯方向分量$\mathcal L$所满足的方程组。获得了$\mathcal L$在局部坐标系下的具体解析表达式,这为后续构造全局扩展提供了关键的技术基础。验证了该解与平坦性条件的相容性。

Conclusion: 本文为将DHS平坦联络扩展至Teichmüller空间的系列工作奠定了基础,通过局部显式求解全纯方向分量的方程组,完成了扩展方案的关键一步。全局扩展的构造以及相关亚纯联络的扩展将在后续论文中完成。该工作为研究高亏格黎曼曲面上的共形场论、可积系统及其模空间的几何结构提供了新的数学工具。

Abstract: The flat DHS connection $\mathcal J_{\mathrm{DHS}}$ constructed in arXiv:2602.01461 is smooth on the configuration space of $n$ points on a fixed compact Riemann surface $Σ$ of arbitrary genus $h$, takes values in an infinite-dimensional Lie algebra $\hat{\mathfrak t}_{h,n}$ and is invariant under the modular group $\mathrm{Sp}(2h,\mathbb Z)$. This paper is the first in a series for a program whose goal is to extend the connection $\mathcal J_{\mathrm{DHS}}$ to a global flat connection on the Teichmüller space $\mathcal T_{h,n}$ valued in the Lie algebra of derivations of $\hat{\mathfrak t}_{h,n}$. Upon the choice of local coordinates adapted to the map $\mathcal T_{h,n}\to \mathcal T_h$, such a connection splits into three pieces: $\mathcal J_{\mathrm{DHS}}$, a piece $\mathcal L$ corresponding to holomorphic directions of $\mathcal T_h$ and a third piece corresponding to anti-holomorphic directions in $\mathcal T_h$. In this paper, we isolate the system of equations satisfied by $\mathcal L$ and obtain its solution locally and explicitly. The construction of a global extension of $\mathcal J_{\mathrm{DHS}}$ to $\mathcal T_{h,n}$ and the extension of the meromorphic connection of arXiv:1112.0864 to $\mathcal T_{h,n}$, are relegated to future publications in this series.

</details>


### [125] [One-loop renormalization group flow of the translation-invariant noncommutative Yukawa theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29213)
*Karim Bouchachia,Smain Kouadik*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究四维欧几里得Moyal空间上平移不变非对易赝标Yukawa理论的单圈重整化群流。通过解析求解耦合的非线性beta函数系统,证明了理论的单圈可重整性,并发现对称性在紫外恢复、红外放大的动力学行为,以及Yukawa诱导的非平面奇异性在红外区域被动力学抑制的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非对易场论中Moyal星乘积的不同排序会产生多个独立耦合常数,需要研究这些耦合常数的重整化群流动行为,特别是探究非对易Yukawa理论的可重整性、对称性演化规律以及非平面结构的红外奇异性问题。

Method: 采用单圈重整化群分析方法,对两个独立耦合常数g₁和g₂构成的非线性beta函数系统进行解析求解。通过引入变量u=g₁²+g₂²和v=g₁²-g₂²实现系统解耦,在对称面g₁=g₂上利用Riccati约化求解四次耦合,并利用精确不变量𝓘=(g₁g₂)³/(g₁²-g₂²)⁴将一般非对称流简化为单一求积问题。同时在对称临界轨迹上以闭合形式求解量纲扇形(M²,m,a²)。

Result: 证明了理论的单圈可重整性——所有单圈发散项都被作用量中已有的反项吸收。发现比值r=v/u存在紫外吸引的对称面r=0和红外吸引的非对称方向r=±1,流动在紫外方向恢复两种Moyal排序间的对称性,在红外方向则以对数的分数幂形式放大初始排序不对称性。Yukawa诱导的非平面1/(θ²p²)结构系数在p→0时被动力学抑制,但奇异性本身未被消除。

Conclusion: 四维非对易赝标Yukawa理论在单圈层次具有可重整性。重整化群流展现出独特的动力学特征:Moyal排序对称性的紫外恢复与红外破缺,以及非平面奇异性系数的动力学抑制。这些结果揭示了非对易场论中星乘积排序的物理意义和能标依赖行为,为理解非对易几何的量子效应提供了理论基础。非平面红外奇异性的完全消除仍需引入红外改善项。

Abstract: We study the one-loop renormalization group flow of the translation-invariant noncommutative pseudoscalar Yukawa theory on four-dimensional Euclidean Moyal space. The two inequivalent orderings of the Moyal-star Yukawa vertex give rise to two independent couplings $g_{1}$ and $g_{2}$, whose beta functions form a coupled nonlinear system; every one-loop divergence is absorbed by a counterterm already present in the action, establishing one-loop renormalizability of the theory. We solve the one-loop system analytically: it decouples in the variables $u=g_{1}^{2}+g_{2}^{2}$ and $v=g_{1}^{2}-g_{2}^{2}$, the quartic coupling is obtained by a Riccati reduction on the symmetric surface $g_{1}=g_{2}$, and the generic asymmetric flow is reduced to a single quadrature by the exact invariant $\mathcal{I}=(g_{1}g_{2})^{3}/(g_{1}^{2}-g_{2}^{2})^{4}$. Two dynamical consequences follow. The ratio $r=v/u$ has a UV-attractive symmetric surface $r=0$ and IR-attractive asymmetric directions $r=\pm1$, so that the flow restores the symmetry between the two Moyal orderings towards the ultraviolet and amplifies any initial ordering asymmetry towards the infrared, as a fractional power of the logarithm. In consequence the Yukawa-induced coefficient of the non-planar $1/(θ^{2}p^{2})$ structure is dynamically suppressed towards $p\rightarrow 0$; the singularity itself is not removed, and remains the task of the IR-improving term. We also solve the dimensionful sector ($M^{2}$, $m$, $a^{2}$) in closed form on the symmetric critical trajectory, and compare throughout with the commutative theory.

</details>


### [126] [Inequalities and Positivity in Modular Flowed Entanglement Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29329)
*Liangyu Chen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究模哈密顿量生成的模流演化下的纠缠熵变化，定义了模纠缠偏移（MES）。文章证明了对于二维共形场论真空中的两个不相交区间，全局共形对合对称性强制两个MES相等且非负，并将模流轨道与Connes余循环轨道联系起来，探讨了在AdS/CFT对应中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模哈密顿量在量子信息论、量子场论和AdS/CFT对应中具有重要作用，但其生成的模流演化下纠缠熵的动力学行为尚不清楚。与固定量子通道演化不同，模流演化不遵循要求纠缠熵变化具有确定符号的一般原理。因此需要研究这种特殊动力学设置下纠缠熵变化（模纠缠偏移）的性质，特别是其符号特征和对称性约束。

Method: 1) 从参考态的约化密度矩阵张量积出发，在参考态自身生成的模流下演化该乘积态；2) 定义并计算给定区域的纠缠熵变化（模纠缠偏移MES）；3) 对于A和B的二分系统，建立精确的熵平衡关系；4) 针对二维共形场论真空中的两个不相交区间，利用全局共形对合变换的对称性进行分析；5) 使用共形传输正规化方法和基于Araki相对熵的内禀方法双重证明；6) 将模流轨道与Connes余循环轨道建立联系。

Result: 建立了精确的熵平衡关系：子系统A和B的MES之和等于沿轨道产生的互信息，因此其和非负（尽管单个MES可能为负）。对于二维共形场论真空中两个不相交区间的情况，证明了交换区间的全局共形对合强制两个MES相等，从而得出沿模流演化每个MES都非负的结论。该结果通过共形传输正规化和Araki相对熵两种方法得到证实。成功识别了模流轨道与Connes余循环轨道的对应关系。

Conclusion: 模纠缠偏移（MES）作为模流演化下纠缠熵变化的度量，展现出独特的动力学性质。虽然一般情况下单个MES符号不定，但通过对称性约束（如二维CFT中的共形对合）可以保证其非负性。A和B子系统的MES之和总是非负的，等于生成的互信息。模流轨道与Connes余循环轨道的联系为理解量子纠缠的代数结构提供了新视角，并为AdS/CFT对应中的全息纠缠熵研究开辟了可能的应用方向。

Abstract: The modular Hamiltonian plays an important role in quantum information theory, quantum field theory, and the AdS/CFT correspondence. In this paper, we study a specific dynamical setup: starting from the tensor product of the reduced density matrices of a reference state, we evolve this product state under the modular flow generated by the reference state itself. We then investigate the resulting change in the entanglement entropy of a given region, which we call the modular entanglement shift (MES). Unlike evolution under a fixed quantum channel, this modular evolution obeys no general principle requiring the MES to have a definite sign. For a bipartition into subsystems A and B, we establish an exact entropy balance: the sum of the MESs for A and B equals the mutual information generated along the orbit. Consequently, their sum is nonnegative, even though either individual shift may be negative. For two disjoint intervals in the vacuum of a two-dimensional conformal field theory, however, we prove that a global conformal involution exchanging the intervals forces the two MESs to be equal. It follows that each MES is nonnegative along the modular flow. We establish this result both using a conformally transported regulator and intrinsically in terms of Araki relative entropy. Finally, we identify the modular-flow orbit with a Connes cocycle orbit and discuss possible applications to the AdS/CFT correspondence.

</details>


### [127] [Bound States in Perturbative Quantum Gravity with Hydrogen-like Degeneracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29402)
*Callum R. T. Jones,Shruti Paranjape,Marcos Skowronek*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在长程相互作用有效场论框架下,交换不同自旋(0, 1/2, 1, 3/2, 2)无质量介质粒子的双体束缚态中隐藏的Laplace-Runge-Lenz(LRL)矢量守恒条件,发现经典层面轨道不进动是普遍现象,但量子层面维持束缚态简并性需要引力子与6个Majorana引力微子之间的特殊抵消,暗示N=6超引力理论的特殊地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力散射振幅的最新研究表明某些高度对称的相对论系统可能在双体束缚态中精确守恒LRL矢量。为了深入理解这一现象,需要系统研究不同自旋无质量介质粒子交换下LRL矢量守恒的条件,特别是在经典(无轨道进动)和量子(类氢原子束缚态简并)两个层面的要求,以揭示可能存在的深层对称性和理论约束。

Method: 在长程相互作用的通用有效场论框架下,考虑自旋为0, 1/2, 1, 3/2, 2的无质量介质粒子交换,系统研究隐藏LRL矢量的守恒条件。技术方法包括:(1)将相对论后闵可夫斯基势匹配方法推广到量子修正;(2)纳入成对无质量费米子交换导致的长程力贡献;(3)分别在经典和量子层面检验轨道进动消失和束缚态能级简并的条件。

Result: 研究发现:(1)在经典层面,包括Kaluza-Klein理论在内的大量包含矢量和标量交换的模型都普遍表现出无轨道进动的性质;(2)在量子层面,维持束缚态简并性需要引力子与6个Majorana引力微子之间发生令人惊讶的精确抵消;(3)这种特殊的抵消机制强烈暗示N=6超引力理论具有特殊作用。

Conclusion: 本文通过系统的有效场论分析揭示了LRL矢量守恒的经典和量子条件之间存在显著差异:经典无进动是广泛存在的普遍现象,但量子简并性的维持则需要极为特殊的理论结构。引力子与6个Majorana引力微子之间必需的精确抵消指向N=6超引力理论的独特地位,为理解引力散射振幅中观察到的隐藏对称性提供了重要线索,也为探索高能物理中的超对称理论提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Recent results in the study of gravitational scattering amplitudes indicate that some highly-symmetric relativistic systems may exactly conserve a version of the Laplace-Runge-Lenz (LRL) vector in two-body bound states. We make a systematic study, in the context of a generic EFT of long-range interactions due to the exchange of massless mediator particles of spins 0, $\frac{1}{2}$, 1, $\frac{3}{2}$, 2, of the conditions for the conservation of a hidden LRL vector; both classically (no orbital precession) and quantum mechanically (hydrogen-like degeneracy of bound states). The calculations require several new technical developments including the extension of relativistic post-Minkowskian potential matching to quantum corrections and the incorporation of long-range forces due to the exchange of pairs of massless fermions. We find that while classically the absence of precession is a generic property of a large class of models, including Kaluza-Klein theories, with vector and scalar exchanges, maintaining the degeneracy quantum mechanically requires a surprising cancellation between gravitons and 6 Majorana gravitinos, hinting at a special role for $\mathcal{N}=6$ supergravity.

</details>


### [128] [Teleparallel formulation of $E_{8(8)}$ Exceptional Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29541)
*Davide Rovere*

TL;DR: 本文在远平行表述框架下推导了$E_{8(8)} \times \mathbb{R}^+$例外场论的势能,该理论适用于研究高维超引力的三维最大超对称紧化,通过要求对偶群最大紧子群的无穷小旋转不变性来选择势能形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维超引力的紧化研究需要一个能够在几何层面明确体现本征挠率核心作用的理论框架。传统方法未能充分展现本征挠率与规范超引力嵌入张量之间的深层联系,因此需要在远平行表述下重新推导例外场论的势能。

Method: 从本征挠率的二次标量缩并的最一般组合出发,构造在广义微分同胚下流形不变的ansatz;通过要求势能在对偶群$E_{8(8)}$的最大紧子群$SO(16)$的无穷小旋转变换下保持不变性来筛选和确定势能的具体形式;采用远平行几何表述使本征挠率的几何角色显式化。

Result: 成功在远平行表述框架下推导出$E_{8(8)} \times \mathbb{R}^+$例外场论的势能;建立了本征挠率与规范超引力嵌入张量之间的明确对应关系;该表述在几何层面清晰展现了本征挠率在高维超引力紧化研究中的核心地位。

Conclusion: 远平行表述为研究三维最大超对称紧化提供了一个几何上更为透明的框架,使本征挠率作为规范超引力嵌入张量的物理意义得以在几何层面显式体现。这一推导验证了远平行几何方法在例外场论和超引力紧化研究中的有效性,为理解高维超引力的低维有效理论提供了新的几何视角。

Abstract: The potential of $E_{8(8)} \times \mathbb{R}^+$ exceptional field theory, suited for studying three-dimensional maximally supersymmetric compactifications of higher-dimensional supergravities, is derived in teleparallel formulation. This means that, starting from an ansatz of the most general combination of the quadratic scalar contractions of the intrinsic torsion, which is manifestly invariant under generalised diffeomorphisms, one selects the potential by imposing the invariance under infinitesimal rotation of the maximal compact subgroup of the duality group. The teleparallel formulation makes manifest at geometric level the preeminent role of the intrinsic torsion in studying compactifications of higher-dimensional supergravities, where the intrinsic torsion is identified with the embedding tensor of the resulting gauged supergravity.

</details>


### [129] [Higher-Spin dS/CFT beyond minimal type-A, and the doubled nature of de Sitter space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29594)
*Julian Lang,Yasha Neiman*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了4维de Sitter空间中自由矢量模型与高自旋引力之间的全息对偶关系，引入新的实性条件以包含奇数自旋并将对偶从A型扩展到B型，发现A型和B型理论可在"平行宇宙"中共存或作为同一扭量空间理论的Legendre变换描述，从而调和了de Sitter空间的双边界性质与欧氏AdS的单边界性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的高自旋全息对偶在4维de Sitter空间中存在局限性：无法包含奇数自旋、仅限于A型理论(边界标量场)、以及de Sitter空间的双边界结构与欧氏AdS单边界结构之间存在表观矛盾。需要发展新的理论框架来克服这些限制，建立更完整的高自旋全息对偶理论。

Method: 在体和边界两侧引入新的实性条件，这些条件与de Sitter空间中单粒子(超)多重态的高自旋对称Hilbert空间范数密切相关。构建两种互补的理论视角：(1)"平行宇宙"图景，其中A型和B型理论分别对应de Sitter空间的偶数和奇数对映鉴别；(2)Legendre变换图景，将A型和B型视为同一欧氏AdS扭量空间理论的Legendre变换描述，利用高自旋配分函数自对偶的性质。

Result: 成功将全息对偶从A型(边界标量)扩展到B型(边界旋量)，并在体理论中包含了奇数自旋。建立了A型和B型理论的两种等价描述方式，揭示了它们在de Sitter空间不同对映鉴别下的共存关系，以及作为扭量空间理论Legendre对偶的统一性。然而，试图通过引入体费米子来统一A型和B型的超对称理论在适当的实性条件下不自洽。

Conclusion: 通过引入新的实性条件和构建双重理论视角，本研究显著扩展了4维de Sitter空间中的高自旋全息对偶理论，成功调和了de Sitter空间的双边界结构与欧氏AdS单边界结构之间的表观矛盾。这一框架将对偶推广到包含奇数自旋和B型理论，但超对称扩展在当前实性条件下遇到了一致性障碍，这为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: We study the holographic duality between free vector models and Higher-Spin Gravity in 4-dimensional de Sitter space. On both the bulk and boundary sides, we introduce new reality conditions, which allow us to include odd spins in the bulk, and to extend the duality from type-A to type-B (i.e. from boundary scalars to spinors). The new reality conditions are closely related to a higher-spin-symmetric Hilbert-space norm for the single-particle (super)multiplet in de Sitter space. We construct two alternative viewpoints on the type-A and type-B theories. In one view, the two co-exist in "parallel universes", specifically in even vs. odd antipodal identifications of de Sitter space. In the other view, type-A and type-B are Legendre-transformed descriptions of the same twistor-space theory in Euclidean AdS (this involves the observation that the higher-spin partition function is Legendre-dual to itself). In this way, higher-spin holography reconciles the two-boundary nature of de Sitter space with the one-boundary nature of Euclidean AdS. The supersymmetric theory that unifies type-A and type-B with bulk fermions fails to be consistent under the appropriate reality conditions.

</details>


### [130] [Genuine Multi-Entropy in Abelian Chern--Simons Theory: Exact Key-Ring Collapse and Its Breakdown for Generic Link States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29627)
*Yen-Cheng Chang,Kaberi Goswami,Norihiro Iizuka,Akihiro Miyata*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究阿贝尔$U(1)_k$陈-西蒙斯理论中的真正多体熵。对于钥匙环链态，导出了$\mathtt{q}$-部分Rényi多体熵的精确闭式表达式。研究发现对于$\mathtt{q}=4$，真正多体熵完全塌缩到三部分信息；对于一般四组分链态，数值扫描揭示了塌缩现象对Rényi指数$n$和陈-西蒙斯能级$k$的算术结构存在非平凡的联合依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体纠缠的量化在量子信息和拓扑量子场论中具有重要意义。尽管双部分和三部分纠缠已被广泛研究，但对于更高阶多体纠缠（特别是真正多体熵）的解析理解仍然有限。阿贝尔陈-西蒙斯理论提供了一个可处理的平台来精确研究多体纠缠结构，特别是链态的拓扑纠缠性质。本文旨在系统研究不同链态构型下真正多体熵的精确行为及其与系统参数的依赖关系。

Method: 1. 针对钥匙环链态（其中只有一个核心组分$K$与其余$\mathtt{q}-1$个组分存在非零链环数），解析推导了任意$\mathtt{q}$、能级$k$和Rényi指数$n$下的$\mathtt{q}$-部分Rényi多体熵的精确闭式表达式。2. 分析了$\mathtt{q}=4$和$\mathtt{q}=5$情况下真正多体熵与低阶纠缠测度的关系。3. 超越钥匙环类，对具有任意成对链环数的一般四组分链态进行研究。4. 对陈-西蒙斯能级$2\leq k\leq24$范围内进行系统的数值扫描，检验不同Rényi指数$n=2,3,4,5$下塌缩现象的保持或破坏情况。5. 分析塌缩破坏与能级$k$的算术结构（如3-进赋值、零因子结构等）及Rényi指数的联合依赖关系。

Result: 1. 对于钥匙环链态：导出了精确的解析表达式；当$\mathtt{q}=4$时，真正多体熵$\mathrm{GM}^{(4)}_n$在所有$n$下完全塌缩到三部分信息$I_{3,n}$；当$\mathtt{q}=5$时，同样由三部分和双部分Rényi多体熵的线性组合完全确定。2. 对于一般四组分链态：钥匙环态中发现的全$n$塌缩对一般链态不再成立；在$n=2$时，对所有检验的能级，塌缩保持精确；在$n=3$时，仅当$3\mid k$时出现破坏，且依赖于$k$的3-进赋值；在$n=4$和$n=5$时，所有检验的能级都出现破坏，破坏率随$k$强烈变化。

Conclusion: 研究表明真正多体熵在阿贝尔陈-西蒙斯理论中展现出丰富的结构。对于特殊的钥匙环链态，可以获得精确的解析结果，显示高阶多体熵可由低阶纠缠测度完全确定。然而对于一般链态，这种塌缩关系的破坏呈现出复杂的模式：不是简单由复合环$\mathbb{Z}_k$的零因子结构控制，而是表现出对Rényi指数和陈-西蒙斯能级算术结构的非平凡联合依赖。特别是$n=2$时塌缩的普遍保持以及$n=3$时与能级整除性相关的选择性破坏，揭示了拓扑纠缠在不同参数区间的微妙行为，为理解多体量子纠缠的层次结构提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We study genuine multi-entropy in Abelian $U(1)_k$ Chern-Simons theory. For key-ring link states, where only the linking numbers between one distinguished component $K$ and the remaining $\mathtt{q}-1$ components are nonzero, we derive an exact closed-form expression for the $\mathtt{q}$-partite Rényi multi-entropy for general $\mathtt{q}$, level $k$, and Rényi index $n$. For $\mathtt{q}=4$, this shows that the genuine multi-entropy $\mathrm{GM}^{(4)}_n$ collapses exactly onto the tripartite information $I_{3,n}$ for all $n$, while for $\mathtt{q}=5$ it is likewise completely determined, for all $n$, by a linear combination of tripartite and bipartite Rényi multi-entropies. We then go beyond the key-ring class and study general four-component link states with arbitrary pairwise linking numbers. A numerical scan over Chern--Simons levels $2\leq k\leq24$ shows that the all-$n$ collapse found analytically for key-ring states does not survive for generic link states. Remarkably, the collapse remains exact at $n=2$ for every level examined. At $n=3$, violations occur, within the scanned range, only when $3\mid k$, with a further dependence on the $3$-adic valuation of $k$. At $n=4$ and $n=5$, violations occur for every level examined, with rates that vary strongly with $k$. These results show that the breakdown is not controlled simply by the zero-divisor structure of composite $\mathbb Z_k$, but instead exhibits a nontrivial joint dependence on the Rényi index and the arithmetic structure of the Chern--Simons level.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 潜在几何为理解BFSS/BMN模型中几何相变提供了新视角,证明了紧急几何信息可以通过例外的N=2扇区在几何相变前被编码和保存,同一量子系统可以承载不同时空结构的解码,揭示了时空变换的可能性和洛伦兹时间涌现的机制

Abstract: We introduce \emph{latent geometry} in BFSS/BMN models. Before an emergent geometry evaporates at the geometric transition, large-$d$ Gaussianization encodes its information in the exceptional $N=2$ sector selected by the baby fuzzy sphere, a single active spin-$1/2$ Pauli block. Dimensional reduction, finite-$N$ Gaussianization and exact Molien--Weyl projection transport this encoding to mass-deformed BFSS$_2$. At $N=2$, the same stable BFSS$_2$ oscillator admits inequivalent unitary $\mathbf{H}^2_θ=\mathbf{EAdS}^2_θ$ and $\mathbf{dS}^2_θ$ decodings, realizing spacetime transmutation and the emergence of Lorentzian time.

</details>


### [132] [One-Loop Fluctuation Response Along a Constrained Noncommutative Modulus in the Lorentzian IIB Matrix Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29688)
